## Achievement/Trophy Guide

The Witcher III: Wild Hunt has a total of 52 achievements and 53 trophies to unlock. As with any RPG experience these are awarded for experiencing the game's lengthy story campaign, checking out some of the game's many side-missions, mastering the Gwent mini-game, exploring the meticulously crafted world and undergoing some additional miscellaneous tasks.

Below you'll find a categorized list of the achievements/trophies in the game and how you can unlock each one.

### Story-Related Achievements/Trophies

The following 8 achievements/trophies will all come with story progression, so as long as you finish the game these should be pretty much un-missable. The only exception to this is the **‘Full Crew'** achievement/trophy which is! It requires that you recruit all possible allies to Kaer Morhen during Act 2, which requires completion of a number of optional secondary quests MISSABLE (you'll find a more detailed explanation of what you need to do to earn this achievement/trophy below the table).

Here's a list of said achievements/trophies in order of appearance:

| Name | Unlock |
| --- | --- |
| Lilac and Gooseberries | Complete the **‘Lilac and Gooseberries'** quest. |
| Family Counsellor | Complete the **‘Bloody Baron'** and **‘Family Matters'** quests. |
| A Friend in Need | Complete the **‘A Poet Under Pressure'** quest. |
| Necromancer | Complete the **‘Nameless'** quest. |
| Something More | Complete the **‘Isle of Mists'** quest. |
| Xenonaut | Complete the **‘Through Time and Space'** quest. |
| The King is Dead | Complete the **‘On Thin Ice'** quest. |

##### Full Crew

To earn this achievement/trophy, you will need to recruit all possible allies for Kaer Morhen during the four **‘Brothers in Arms'** quests that occur prior to the end of Act 2. The allies you recruit include the following:

- **Keira Metz –** Complete her side-missions ‘The Magic Lamp', ‘An Invitation from Keira Metz', ‘A Towerful of Mice', ‘A Favour for a Friend' and ‘For the Advancement of Learning'. During the final quest's dialogue, convince her to go to Kaer Morhen to recruit her. Allowing her to go on her own way or killing her will nullify the achievement/trophy.
- **Triss Merigold –** Complete her side-missions ‘A Matter of Life and Death' and ‘Now or Never'.
- **Vernon Roche -** Complete his side-mission ‘An Eye for an Eye' and then visit him at the Temerian Partisan Camp and request that he comes to Kaer Morhen.
- **Zoltan Chevay –** Speak with Zoltan and ask him to come to Kaer Morhen and he'll go immediately. You should do his ‘A Dangerous Game' quest first though as it has unique, missable Gwent cards.
- **Hjalmar an Craite** – Complete the Skellige side-quests ‘The Lord of Undvik', ‘Possession', ‘King's Gambit' and ‘Coronation'. No matter who you side with during the final quest: Cerys or Hjalmar, in both cases Hjalmar will come to Kaer Morhen if asked.
- **Ermion** – Speak with Ermion on Skellige and ask him to come to Kaer Morhen and he'll go immediately.

### Game Completion Achievements/Trophies

These three achievements will unlock once you complete the game on the specified difficulty. Please note if you do complete the game on the Death March difficulty, it will also unlock the achievements/trophies for the lesser difficulties. If you do one run through the game on the Death March difficulty you can earn all three at once.

| Name | Unlock |
| --- | --- |
| Passed the Trial | Finish the game on any difficulty. |
| Ran the Gauntlet | Finish the game on the "Blood and Broken Bones!" or "Death March!" difficulty levels. |
| Walked the Path | Finish the game on the "Death March!" difficulty level. |

### Secondary Quest Achievements/Trophies

There are 14 achievements/trophies tied into the secondary quests available in The Witcher III: Wild Hunt and these have been included most likely to encourage players to explore the game's world to experience everything the title has to offer. Each of them are fairly self-explanatory, but can be tricky to locate as the game never points these side-tasks out to you.

There are 8 side-quest achievements/trophies associated with the Witcher Contracts that can be picked up from notice boards around the world and a further 3 achievements/trophies are available for narrative-driven side-quests involving characters from the main story. The final 3 achievements/trophies come from completing some less involved side-quests.

Fortunately, the side-quest guide above can help you out with the specifics of each. Here are a list of the achievements/trophies that fall into this category and what needs to be done to unlock each of them:

> **Note:** Several of the side-quests associated with achievements/trophies can be failed if you progress too far into the main quest without dealing with them.

#### Witcher Contract Achievements/Trophies

Six of the game's 26 Witcher Contracts will earn you an achievement/trophy upon completion, another trophy/achievement relates to completing 2/26 Witcher Contracts without bonuses, whilst one final achievement/trophy is awarded upon completion of all 26. The achievements/trophies involved include the following:

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Unlock |
| Ashes to Ashes | Complete the **‘Contract: Doors Slamming Shut'** side-quest available from the Heirarch's Square notice board (Novigrad). |
| Fearless Vampire Slayer | Complete the **‘Contract: The Mystery of the Byways Murders'** side-quest available from the Oreton notice board (Velen). |
| Fiend or Foe? | Complete the **‘Contract: Missing Son'** side-quest available from the Rannvaig notice board (Ard Skellige). |
| Shrieker | Complete the **‘Contract: Shrieker'** side-quest available from the Crow's Perch notice board (Velen). |
| The Doppler Effect | Complete the **‘Contract: An Elusive Thief'** side-quest available from the Heirarch's Square notice board (Novigrad). |

##### Geralt: The Professional

**Description:** Complete all 26 Witcher Contracts.

This achievement/trophy requires us to find and complete all 26 (or 25 from patch 1.07) of the Witcher Contracts in the game. There is one in White Orchard, 11 in Velen, eight in Novigrad and six in the Skellige Isles. The Witcher Contracts are the most involved of the side-quests in the game and often feature boss fights against stronger versions of standard monsters, making them trickier than most –fortunately, if you are struggling, I've got you covered with walkthroughs and strategies to each of the contracts above. The Witcher Contracts include:

##### White Orchard

- Contract: The Devil by the Well

##### Velen

- Contract: Jenny O' the Woods
- Contract: Missing Brother
- Contract: Mysterious Tracks
- Contract: Patrol Gone Missing
- Contract: Phantom of the Trade Route
- Contract: Shrieker
- Contract: Swamp Thing
- Contract: The Griffin from the Highlands
- Contract: The Merry Widow
- Contract: The Mystery of the Byways Murders
- Contract: Woodland Beast

##### Novigrad

- Contract: An Elusive Thief
- Contract: Deadly Delights
- Contract: Doors Slamming Shut
- Contract: Lord of the Wood
- Contract: The Apiarian Phantom
- Contract: The Creature from the Oxenfurt Forest
- Contract: The Oxenfurt Drunk
- Contract: The White Lady

##### Skellige

- Contract: Dragon
- Contract: Here Comes the Groom
- Contract: Missing Son
- Contract: Muire D'yaeblen
- Contract: Strange Beast
- Contract: The Phantom of Eldberg

> **Note:** From patch 1.07, the White Orchard Witcher Contract **‘Contract: Devil by the Well'** no longer counts towards this achievement/trophy.

##### Even Odds

**Description:** Kill 2 monsters you have a contract on without using Signs, potions, mutagens, oils or bombs.

To earn this achievement/trophy, you'll need to complete two of the 26 Witcher Contracts without using Signs, Oils, Bombs or Potions/Decoctions. My recommendation would be to leave a few of the lower levelled Contracts undone until you reach the end of the game after which you can return to complete them. By this stage you should have superior stats and weaponry and should be able to defeat the monsters in a relatively short amount of time with a few good swings from a silver sword.

#### Side-quest Achievements/Trophies

The following seven achievements/trophies are tied into the completion of optional side-quests or side-quest chains that can be undertaken at various points during or after the main game.

> **Note:** Several of the side-quests associated with these achievements/trophies can be failed if you progress too far into the main quest without dealing with them.

These side-quests are those which you'll need to complete in order to unlock the associated achievements/trophies. Note that if you need help finding or completing them, walkthroughs for all of these side-quests can be found in the side-quest section above.

| Name | Unlock |
| --- | --- |
| Assassin of Kings | Complete the **‘A Matter of Life and Death', ‘Now or Never', ‘A Deadly Plot'** and **‘Reason of State'** side-quest chain in Novigrad. |
| Friends With Benefits | Complete the **‘The Magic Lamp', ‘An Invitation from Kiera Metz', ‘A Towerful of Mice', ‘A Favour for a Friend'** and **‘For the Advancement of Knowledge'** side-quest chain in Velen. |
| Kingmaker | Complete the **‘Lord of Undvik', ‘Possession', ‘King's Gambit'** and **‘Coronation'** side-quest chain in the Skellige Isles. |
| Woodland Spirit | Complete the ‘ **In the Heart of the Woods**' side-quest available from NPCs by the town of Farylund on Ard Skellig, the Skellige Isles. |
| Brawl Master | Complete the **‘Fists of Fury: Velen', ‘Fists of Fury: Novigrad', ‘Fists of Fury: Skellige' and ‘Fists of Fury: Champion of Champions'** side-quests. |
| Brawler | Complete the **‘Fists of Fury: Skellige' side-** quest. |
| Fast and Furious | Compete in and win all 11 races in the side-quests: ‘ **Races: Crow's Perch**' in Velen (3 Races), **‘The Great Erasmus Vegelbud Memorial Derby'** in Novigrad (4 Races) and **‘Hero's Pursuits'** in the Skellige Isles (4 Races). |

### Crafting and Upgrades Achievements/Trophies

The Witcher III: Wild Hunt has five achievements/trophies that are awarded upon completing various Skill Tree character development and crafting tasks. The majority of these are fairly self-explanatory and easy enough to unlock, but will take time to complete due to the need to harvest plenty of reagents for your crafting endeavors and to earn enough XP to reach level 35 and to have enough skill points completely unlock one of the three Skill Trees.

Here's a list of said achievements/trophies and how to accomplish each:

##### Munchkin

**Description:** Reach character development level 35.

As per your standard RPG experience players will earn experience points (XP) which can be accumulated to earn levels. Upon levelling, Geralt will be given a small boost to his base attributes and unlock a skill point. In order to earn this achievement/trophy, you'll need to have Geralt reach character level 35. You won't be able to do this by solely sticking to the main campaign missions and as such, you'll need to complete as many side-quests as possible and defeat similarly levelled enemies to keep the XP ticking over,

In the Witcher 3 you'll gain XP via the following:

- Killing enemies of a similar level.
- Completing objectives and progressing through story events.
- Completing secondary-quests and Witcher Contracts.
- Completing side-tasks such as: Abandoned Settlements, Monster Nests and Treasure Hunts.
- Using **AXII** during conversations.
- Bribing people during conversations.

##### Dendrologist

**Description:** Acquire all the Abilities in one tree.

Each time Geralt earns sufficient XP to level up, he'll be granted a new Skill Point which can then be invested to unlock one of the skills in a Skill Tree. In addition to earning Skill points upon levelling, you'll also be able to obtain them from discovering and using a Place of Power for the first time – so stay on the lookout for those as you explore the world.

There are three main Skill Trees available to Geralt in The Witcher III: Wild Hunt – ‘Combat Skills', ‘Signs' and ‘Alchemy' and a fourth more broadly focused ‘General Skills' Skill Tree. Each Skill Tree features different skill sets that will enable Geralt to unlock passive bonuses, new abilities or enhanced capabilities.

In order to unlock this achievement/trophy, you'll have to spend enough Skill Points to purchase at least the first tier of every ability available within a single Skill Tree. Each of the ‘Combat Skills', ‘Signs' and ‘Alchemy' have 20 abilities each, whilst the ‘General Skills' Tree has 10. An easy way to unlock this achievement/trophy quickly is to purchase a **Potion of Clearance** to reset Geralt's Skill Points and invest them all into the ‘General Skills' Skills Tree.

##### Mutant

**Description:** Fill all mutagen slots.

Mutagens are objects dropped by monsters throughout the game and they have a number of uses. For the purpose of this achievement/trophy,

In the Character Development section of the main menu, in addition to Skill Trees to spend your hard-earned Skill Points on, you can also apply Mutagens to Geralt for some passive bonuses. There are four Mutagen slots in total with one unlocking after every seven character levels. The fourth and final slot is unlocked unlocked once Geralt reaches character level 28.

Once all four slots are unlocked, simply place a Mutagen in each of the four slots to have the achievement/trophy unlock.

##### ‘Let's Cook' and ‘Bombardier'

**Description:** Learn 12 potion formulae and six different bomb formulae.

As you progress through the game, you'll be able to find, purchase or acquire a range of formulae to expand Geralt's arsenal of Potions, Decoctions, Oils and Bombs. After finding a new recipe, make your way to the Alchemy tab of the main menu to find a list of all of your acquired formulae. From here you can highlight a recipe of choice to learn what ingredients are required to create it. Once you have acquired the necessary ingredients, you will then be able to craft the desired item from the same menu.

Simply find and have 12 different formulae related to Potions in your Alchemy menu list to have the **‘Let's Cook'** achievement/trophy unlock and six different Bomb recipes for **‘Bombardier'**. See the Alchemy section above for information on types of potions, ingredients required and places to find or purchase basic formulae.

### Combat-based Achievements/Trophies

In addition to the focus on exploration and heavy use of dialogue, The Witcher III: Wild Hunt also has a fairly large focus on its entertaining but challenging combat system. As this is the case, there are quite a few achievements/trophies (12 in fact!) that are tied into finding, unlocking and playing around with the game's various weapons and techniques.

Below you'll find a list of all of the combat-related achievements/trophies in the game and how to unlock each of them:

##### Humpty Dumpty

**Description:** Kill 10 opponents by knocking them off somewhere high with the AARD Sign.

This achievement/trophy is fairly self-explanatory – you need to use your AARD sign to launch enemies from high places so that they are killed. The problem with this though is remembering to do it when you are in a position to do so.

This is easiest to achieve whilst fighting enemies near cliffs where you can kite them to the edge, roll around and blast them off with AARD. This can also be achieved in ruins/built up areas by luring enemies up sets of stairs (the higher the better) and hitting them with the AARD attack from the top to send them sprawling down. If it doesn't kill them, repeat until it gets the job done.

As several readers have pointed out, the Bandit Camp by the ‘Devil's Pit' in central Velen is an excellent place to do this due to its multi-tiered wooden platforms and abundance of enemies. Simply pull the enemies to the top of the area before hitting them with AARD to send them into the pit below. As a bonus, as long as you don't loot the Bandit Camp once you have killed them all, you can fast travel to another region and then back again to have them all respawn to repeat the process all over again!

We'll need to accumulate a total of 10 kills using this method in order to unlock this achievement/trophy.

##### The Enemy of My Enemy

**Description:** Use the AXII Sign to force one opponent to kill another. Do this 20 times.

In order to unlock this achievement/trophy you'll need to first unlock the **‘Puppet'** Skill in the **AXII** section of the Signs Skill Tree. This will enable us to cast **AXII** on an enemy to have it attack their friends. Obviously, you'll need to use this skill whilst fighting groups of enemies such as Drowners, Nekkers or human hostiles such as Bandits/Deserters/Pirates.

We'll need to accumulate a total of 20 kills using this method in order to unlock this achievement/trophy.

##### Environmentally Unfriendly

**Description:** Kill 50 opponents using the environment (e.g. swamp gas, insects or objects).

This achievement/trophy is as the description indicates focused around getting you to play around with the environment to help you in killing bad guys. Options for kills in this regard include the following:

- Using **AARD** to knock people from high places to their deaths
- Using **IGNI** to ignite Swamp gas.
- Using **IGNI** to detonate explosive barrels.
- Using the Crossbow to detonate explosive barrels.
- Using **ARRD** to disturb beehives.

We'll need to accumulate a total of 50 kills using a variety of these methods in order to unlock this achievement/trophy.

##### Kaer Morhen Trained

**Description:** Perform 10 effective counterattacks in a row without getting hit or parrying.

A counterattack can be performed by hitting the block button immediately before an enemy hits you. You'll want to try this against a human enemy as they will often attack using swords/clubs and these weapon attacks are much easier to judge/time a counter attack to (Don't try against Shield wielders or Axe weapons though!).

I found the best way to get into a good position to unlock this achievement/trophy was to either approach a single low levelled enemy, or clear out a low levelled Bandit camp or group encounter but leave one fellow alive at the end. At this point you can simply allow him to attack and activate your counters over and over again until you get it. If he is of sufficiently low level he won't do much damage to you at all and you won't need to worry about managing your health all that often.

##### Can't Touch This!

**Description:** Kill 5 foes in a fight without taking damage (except for Toxicity) and without using the QUEN Sign.

This achievement/trophy is awarded as the description suggests, for taking on five (or more) enemies in combat at once and killing at least five of them, in a row without taking any damage. Of course you aren't allowed to use **QUEN** either – that would make things too easy!

This can be unlocked at any point during the game, but by far the easiest way to earn it is to travel back to the lower levelled areas once you have levelled up a little so that you can one-hit-kill most enemies. Find a group of low levelled enemies (Nekkers/Drowners/Wolves/Bandits) – I'd suggest the level 1-5 enemies in White Orchard - and use all of your fancy sword skills, bombs and signs to take them down without copping any damage in return.

##### That is the Evilest Thing

**Description:** Ignite the gas produced by a Dragon's Dream bomb using a burning opponent. Do this 10 times.

The description for this achievement/trophy sounds like this may be difficult to do, but in reality it's quick and simple to unlock once you know what you are doing. Firstly you'll need to unlock the Dragon's Dream Bomb recipe, either from finding it in a chest or purchasing from a vendor (The herbalist to the east of Oxenfurt had it for me!). After acquiring the recipe and creating it in the Alchemy menu you'll then be able to equip it.

With the Dragon's Dream Bombs equipped, head on into combat and use IGNI to set an enemy on fire. Once successful, simply toss a dragon's Dream Bomb at the enemy to cause a large explosion. Do this a further 9 times to unlock the achievement/trophy!

##### Butcher of Blaviken

**Description:** Kill at least 5 opponents in under 10 seconds.

Killing five enemies within 10 seconds sounds tough, especially when most enemies at a similar level to Geralt will give him a run for his money if you aren't on top of your dodging/attacking game. Fortunately though, there is an easy way to earn this one.

This can be unlocked at any point during the game, but by far the easiest way to earn it is to travel back to the lower levelled areas once you have levelled up a little so that you can one-hit-kill most enemies. Find a group of low levelled enemies (Nekkers/Drowners/Wolves/Bandits) – I'd suggest the level 1-5 enemies in White Orchard - and use all of your fancy sword skills, bombs and signs to take them down as quickly as possible.

##### Overkill

**Description:** Make an opponent suffer from bleeding, poisoning and burning simultaneously. Do this 10 times.

This achievement/trophy is one of the trickier ones to set up as trying to place three different status effects on an enemy within the short window in which they are active can be difficult. Fortunately, with the right equipment and gear setup, Geralt is able to do this without too much of a fuss.

To do this you'll first want to acquire some Broadhead Bolts for your crossbow for the Bleeding debuff. Next you'll want to acquire the recipe for, craft and then equip both Dancing Star Bombs (for the Burning debuff) and Devil's Puffball Bombs (for the Poison debuff). Once you have crafted and equipped all of this gear, you'll want to approach an enemy, hit him/her with a Broadhead Bolt from the crossbow to lead off before quickly following up with the two bomb types to add the additional debuffs. Do this a total of 10 times to earn the achievement/trophy.

##### Master Marksman

**Description:** Kill 50 human and nonhuman opponents by striking them in the head with a crossbow bolt.

You'll receive the crossbow right before the boss fight at the end of the White Orchard prologue area. Simply use it throughout the game to kill enemies with headshots. If you are not one to use the crossbow all that often, try revisiting lower levelled areas once you have moved on and clear out Bandit Camps it should do increased damage to lower-levelled foes.

Although it's not necessary, if you want to grind this one out it's a good idea to stick some Skill points in the archery section of the ‘Combat Skills' Skill Tree - specifically ‘Lightning Reflexes' and ‘Anatomical Knowledge' in order to increase damage.

We'll need to accumulate a total of 50 kills using a variety of these methods in order to unlock this achievement/trophy.

##### Fist of the South Star

**Description:** Defeat an opponent in a fistfight without taking any damage.

There are numerous places to undertake this during the game. There are several unavoidable fist fights during the main campaign quest line and multiple additional fist fights available during the game's four **‘Fists of Fury'** side-quests. You simply need to complete one fist fight at any time without taking damage from the opponent.

##### Triple Threat

**Description:** Kill 3 opponents in one fight using 3 different methods (swords, bombs, crossbow, Signs, etc.).

As the description implies, you'll need to enter into combat against a group of enemies and kill three of them with at least three different types of attacks. It's a good idea to whittle down an opponent's health before finishing them off with a face full of IGNI, a bomb or a crossbow bolt. As long as the finishing blows are from three different attack types, you should earn the achievement/trophy.

##### What was That?

**Description:** Attack, counter, cast a Sign and throw a bomb (in any order) in under 4 seconds.

Whilst this achievement/trophy does sound a little difficult and complicated from its description, it really isn't. Once we have Geralt set up properly – make sure he has a Bomb equipped in his item slot) we simply need to hit a four button sequence in order to unlock this one. I'd recommend doing this against a human opponent so that countering is much easier to do. A simple method to do this is the following:

Approach an armed enemy and bait him into attacking before hitting the block button to counter him. Once countered, immediately hit the quick attack button and then toss a bomb and one of your Signs out as quickly as possible.

This should unlock the achievement/trophy although it may take a few attempts to decrease the total time that it takes you to squeeze out all four actions. Fortunately, we can meditate to restore our bombs as many times as we like to retry!

### Gwent Achievements/Trophies

As you may have figured, a game with such a detailed and entertaining mini-game as Gwent in it would not be complete without some Gwent-related achievement/trophies in it to make things more difficult. The Witcher III: Wild Hunt contains four Gwent-centric trophies/achievements that will have you winning the major in-game tournament, securing victory in unique ways and collecting all of the available Gwent cards for Geralt's collection.

Below you'll find a list of the four Gwent achievements/trophies in the game and a brief description as to how to unlock each of them:

##### Card Collector

**Description:** Acquire all Gwent cards available in the base version of the game.

There are a total of 120 unique cards (without duplicates being included) in the base game and you'll need to collect all 120 of them in order to earn this achievement/trophy. This is easily the longest, most tricky achievement/trophy in the game due to having to keep track of which cards you have, which ones you need and which merchants/players you have defeated and those who you still need to beat.

Some basic information can be found below about how to go about collecting all of the Gwent Cards:

- First of all there are 10 missable Gwent cards, so make sure you keep an eye out for these as you progress through the game. If you miss obtaining these during your play through, you'll be unable to earn this achievement/trophy and will need to either load an earlier save file or start afresh and try again.
- Play and with the Gwent Tournament at the masquerade ball during Triss's **‘A Matter of Life and Death'** side-quest. There are three missable Gwent cards to be won.
- Complete Zoltan's side-quest **‘A Dangerous Game'** before sending him to Kaer Morhen. There are three missable Gwent cards to be found here.
- Win the Gwent Tournament at the Passiflora during the ‘ **High Stakes'** side-quest. If you lose, you'll be locked out of challenging the remaining players and will miss out on the unique cards on offer so be sure to save before you attempt this one! There are four missable Gwent cards to be won.
- Although it appears that beating the Bloody Baron in Gwent is missable if you complete the **‘Return to Crookback Bog'** side-quest. If you return to his office in the keep at Crow's Perch, you'll find his unique Gwent Card on the desk.

You'll also earn yourself a total of 21 unique Gwent cards through finding and completing the various Gwent-related quests in the game:

- **Gwent: Velen Players –** There are four unique Gwent Cards for completing this side-quest.
- **Gwent: Playing Innkeeps** – There are three unique Gwent Cards for completing this side-quest.
- **Gwent: Big City Players** – There are four unique Gwent Cards for completing this side-quest.
- **Gwent: Old Pals –** There are four unique Gwent Cards for completing this side-quest.
- **Gwent: Skellige Style –** There are five unique Gwent Cards for completing this side-quest.
- You'll be rewarded with the unique ‘Iorveth' card for completing the **‘Shock Therapy'** side-quest.

In general, for the remaining Gwent Cards that you are missing, you can also earn additional Cards from the following actions:

- Geralt will start with a Northern Realms deck with 20 unique cards and several duplicates.
- Gwent Cards can be purchased from innkeepers or Traders. It's a good idea to purchase everything available, even if you already own the cards to give you more options with deck building.
- There are a further 52 Cards that can be earned by playing innkeepers, traders and other challengers. You'll earn a new, random card the first time you beat each of them.

##### Gwent Master

**Description:** Defeat Tybalt and win the Gwent tournament held at the Passiflora.

This achievement/trophy requires that we successfully complete the ‘Gwent: High Stakes' side-quest. This involves four Gwent games against highly skilled and well decked players in a tournament held at the Passiflora Brothel in Novigrad. You'll need to have some excellent cards, a good deck and a solid grasp of the game of Gwent before even venturing near this side-quest.

It is also a good idea to save before attempting it as if you lose to any of the four players, the quest will fail and you'll be unable to try again. For a guide to this side quest check out these links in the guide above:

- **[Gwent: High Stakes Walkthrough](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/xboxone/702759-the-witcher-3-wild-hunt/faqs/71896#High%20Stakes)**
- **[Gwent Guide and general strategies](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/xboxone/702759-the-witcher-3-wild-hunt/faqs/71896#Gwent%20Guide)**

##### Geralt and Friends

**Description:** Win a round of Gwent using only neutral cards.

**In Gwent, the n** eutral cards feature Hero/Unit cards that do not belong to a faction. These Gwent Cards include the following:

- **Avallac'h** – From the **‘Gwent: Skellige Style'** side-quest.
- **Dandelion** – From the **‘A Matter of Life and Death'** side quest's Gwent Tournament.
- **Emiel Regis Rohellec Terzieff** – Random award for beating a merchant/trader/innkeeper.
- **Zoltan Chivay** – Random award for beating a merchant/trader/innkeeper.
- **Vesemir** – From the **‘Gwent: Big City Players'** side-quest.
- **Villentretenmerth** – Random award for beating a merchant/trader/innkeeper.
- **Triss Merigold** – From the **‘Gwent: Old Pals'** side-quest.
- **Yennefer of Vengerberg** – From the **‘Gwent: Playing Innkeeps'** side-quest.
- **Cirilla Fiona Elen Rianno** – From the **‘Gwent: Big City Players'** side-quest.
- **Geralt of Rivia** – From the **‘Gwent: Old Pals'** side-quest.

Other Neutral cards include the for type of Weather Cards (Biting Frost, Torrential Rain, Impenetrable Fog and Clear Weather) and the special one-off use cards (Commander's Horn, Scorch, Decoy).

As the neutral hero cards are quite powerful, it should not be too tricky to win a round with several in play at once. The trick to winning a round using only Neutral cards is to first build up a collection of them and stick as many of them into a deck as possible to increase your chance of drawing them. If you don't have them all right away, use some Spy cards (or other drawing abilities) to draw additional cards to your hand until they appear.

##### All In

**Description:** Play three hero cards in one round of Gwent and win the match.

Each faction has its own set of hero cards with Northern Realms, Monsters and Nilfgaardian Empire each having for Heroes in the deck whilst the Scoia'tael only have three. There are a further five neutral hero cards. They include:

- Monsters – **Draug** – From the **‘Gwent: Skellige Style'** side-quest.
- Monsters – **Imlerith** – Random award for beating a merchant/trader/innkeeper.
- Monsters - **Leshen** – From the **‘Gwent: Skellige Style'** side-quest.
- Monsters – **Kayran** – Random award for beating a merchant/trader/innkeeper.
- Neutral - **Avallac'h** – From the **‘Gwent: Skellige Style'** side-quest.
- Neutral - **Triss Merigold** – From the **‘Gwent: Old Pals'** side-quest.
- Neutral - **Yennefer of Vengerberg** – From the **‘Gwent: Playing Innkeeps'** side-quest.
- Neutral - **Cirilla Fiona Elen Rianno** – From the **‘Gwent: Big City Players'** side-quest.
- Neutral - **Geralt of Rivia** – From the **‘Gwent: Old Pals'** side-quest.
- Nilfgaardian Empire - **Morvran Voorhis** – From the **‘Gwent: Big City Players'** side-quest.
- Nilfgaardian Empire - **Tibor Eggebracht** – From the **‘Gwent: Playing Innkeeps'** side-quest.
- Nilfgaardian Empire - **Menno Coehoorn** – From the **‘Gwent: Playing Innkeeps'** side-quest.
- Nilfgaardian Empire - **Letho of Gulet** – From the **‘Gwent: Velen Players'** side-quest.
- Northern Realms - **Esterad Thyssen** – From the **‘Gwent: Big City Players'** side-quest.
- Northern Realms - **Vernon Roche** – From the **‘Gwent: Velen Players'** side-quest.
- Northern Realms - **Philippa Eilhart** – Random award for beating a merchant/trader/innkeeper.
- Northern Realms - **John Natalis** – From the **‘A Dangerous Game'** side-quest.
- Scoia'tael – **Eithné** – From the **‘Gwent: Old Pals'** side-quest.
- Scoia'tael – **Iorveth** – From the **‘Shock Therapy'** side-quest.
- Scoia'tael - **Saesenthessis –** From the **‘Gwent: Old Pals'** side-quest.

As the hero cards are quite powerful, it should not be too tricky to win a round with several in play at once. The trick to winning a round using an abundance of hero cards is to first build up a collection of them in both the base decks and acquiring the neutral heroes on the side. Once you have acquired several of them, you'll want to include as many of them into a deck as possible to increase your chance of drawing them.

Note that you'll need to use three heroes in a single round AND win the game at the end. So be sure that if you go for it early, that you have enough firepower left in your hand/deck to bring home the win in the final rounds.

### Miscellaneous Achievements/Trophies

These final six achievements/trophies are related to the completion of some simple in-game tasks that don't really fall into one of the other categories listed above. They are not particularly difficult, but they can be easy to miss if you don't know what you are looking for. These include (among other things) tasks such as exploring the world to unlock a specific number of fast travel points, destroying Monster nests, completing a Scavenger hunt or two and playing around with Places of Power.

Below you'll find a list of the miscellaneous achievements/trophies and how to unlock each of them:

##### Globetrotter

**Description:** Discover 100 fast travel points.

As you explore the game's massive world, you'll come across fast travel signposts which can be used to, well, fast travel. There are well over 100 of these located around the lands of White Orchard, Velen, Novigrad, Kaer Morhen and the Skellige Isles and you'll earn a significant number of these by simply passing through villages, cities and ruins whilst completing the main campaign and/or side-quests.

If you are having trouble finding additional signposts, try exploring ruins or built up areas on the map that you have not yet explores or work your way around, exploring the ‘?' markers. Once you have 100 of these unlocked, the achievement/trophy will pop for you.

##### ‘Pest Control' and ‘Fire in the Hole'

**Description:** Destroy all Monster Nests in the Velen / Novigrad region, or in Skellige and destroy 10 Monster Nests using bombs.

Monster Nests are one of the side-activities that can be revealed when exploring or uncovering ‘?' markers on the map. These will appear as a dragon head marker on the map once discovered. Monster Nests are often guarded by large groups of monsters which you will need to kill before focusing your attention on the nest. To destroy the nests we can either use Sanum/Grapeshot Bombs or destructive spells such as IGNI.

The **‘Fire in the Hole'** achievement/trophy requires that we destroy 10 such Monster Nests with bombs. The **‘Pest Control'** achievement/trophy requires that we completely wipe out all of the Monster Nests on the Skellige Isles or alternatively those in Novigrad/Velen. As long as we use the bombs to destroy Monster Nests, we can actually do the pair of these achievements/trophies at the same time.

As there is a total of 23 Monster Nests in Novigrad/Velen as opposed to five in the Skellige Isles, it is much quicker to finish off those in Skellige first to quickly net yourself the **‘Pest Control'** achievement/trophy before heading to the mainland and finishing off as many Monster Nests as required with bombs to earn **‘Fire in the Hole'.**

##### Book Worm

**Description:** Read 30 books, journals or other documents.

As you explore the world and loot objects, chests and corpses in the environment you are sure to come across notes and books that Geralt can pick up. Once obtained, head to the inventory menu and hit the action button to register them as read. Once you have done this to a cumulative 30 books/notes the achievement//trophy will unlock.

##### Armed and Dangerous

**Description:** Find and equip all the elements of one set of Witcher gear.

In order to complete the prerequisite for unlocking this achievement/trophy, you'll have a little bit of work on your hands! You'll first need to undertake one of the Scavenger Hunt side-quests for one of the three main schools of armour – Cat, Bear and Griffin. Upon completing a scavenger hunt, you should have picked up 6 Diagrams for a full set of Witcher Gear (Armour, Boots, Gauntlets, Trousers, Silver Sword, Steel Sword).

Once you have the diagrams, make sure that you check your Crafting tab in the menu to locate and/or purchase the ingredients required to create that piece of equipment. With the Diagram and complete set of ingredients in hand, make your way to an armorer and have him craft the items for you. Armorers in Oxenfurt and Novigrad should have the appropriate skill level to undertake the task.

##### Power Overwhelming

**Description:** Have all possible Place of Power bonuses active at the same time.

As the description suggests, you'll need to visit five different Places of Power and draw from them so that all five of your Signs ( **AARD**, **AXII**, **IGNI**, **QUEN** and **YRDEN**) have the increased intensity buff on them at the same time. A Place of Power buff will only remain active for 30 minutes before dissipating, so you'll need to make use of fast travelling and Roach in order to get to the various places of power within the time limit.

Whilst this achievement/trophy is possible elsewhere, by far the easiest place to do this is in White Orchard. This is because the map is much smaller than the other regions and there are more than five Places of Power here and at least one for each of Geralt's different magical signs.
